Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the complex_analysis structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 450. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 557 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on complex_analysis structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 450. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 557. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The complex_analysis variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 448 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 772 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the complex_analysis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 448 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 772. QED.
